 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Item&nbsp;1.01. Entry into a Material Definitive Agreement. </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>Amended and Restated Loan and Security Agreement </U></B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">On April&nbsp;14, 2015, Advanced Micro Devices, Inc., a Delaware corporation (the &#147;Company&#148; or &#147;AMD&#148;), AMD International Sales&nbsp;&amp;
Service, Ltd., a Delaware corporation (together with the Company, the &#147;Borrowers&#148;), and ATI Technologies ULC, an Alberta unlimited liability corporation (together with the Borrowers, the &#147;Loan Parties&#148;), entered into an amended
and restated loan and security agreement (the &#147;Amended and Restated Loan Agreement&#148;) by and among the Loan Parties, the financial institutions party thereto from time to time as lenders (the &#147;Lenders&#148;) and Bank of America, N.A.,
a national banking association, as agent for the lenders party thereto (the &#147;Agent&#148;). The Amended and Restated Loan Agreement amended and restated the loan and security agreement dated as of November&nbsp;12, 2013 by and among the
Borrowers, the financial institutions party thereto from time to time as lenders and the Agent. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Amended and Restated Loan Agreement provides for a
senior secured asset based line of credit for a principal amount up to $500 million (the &#147;Secured Revolving Line of Credit&#148;), with up to $75 million available for issuance of letters of credit. Borrowings under the Secured Revolving Line
of Credit are limited to up to 85% of eligible accounts receivable (90% for certain qualified eligible accounts receivable), minus specified reserves. The size of the commitments under the Secured Revolving Line of Credit may be increased, with the
aggregate amount of all such increases not to exceed $200 million. No drawings were made under the Secured Revolving Line of Credit on the closing date of the Amended and Restated Loan Agreement. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Secured Revolving Line of Credit matures on April&nbsp;14, 2020. The obligations of the Loan Parties under the Amended and Restated Loan Agreement are
secured by a first priority security interest in the Loan Parties&#146; accounts receivable, inventory, deposit accounts maintained with the Agent and other specified assets relating to the foregoing, including books and records. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Borrowers may elect that the loans under the Secured Revolving Line of Credit bear interest at a rate per annum equal to (a)&nbsp;the London Interbank
Offered Rate (&#147;LIBOR&#148;) plus the applicable margin set forth in the applicable chart below (the &#147;Applicable Margin&#148;) as determined by the average availability under the Secured Revolving Line of Credit and the fixed charge
coverage ratio for the most recently ended four-fiscal quarter period, or (b)&nbsp;(i)&nbsp;the greatest of (x)&nbsp;the Agent&#146;s prime rate, (y)&nbsp;the federal funds rate, as published by the Federal Reserve Bank of New York plus 0.50%, and
(z)&nbsp;LIBOR for a one-month period plus 1.00%, plus (ii)&nbsp;the Applicable Margin. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Applicable Margin, if average availability is equal to or greater
than 66.66% of the total commitment amount and the fixed charge coverage ratio for the most recently ended four-fiscal quarter period is greater than or equal to 1.25 to 1.00, is 0.25% for Base Rate Revolver Loans and 1.25% for LIBOR Revolver Loans.
Otherwise, Applicable Margin is determined in accordance with the below table: </P> <P STYLE="font-size:12pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" WIDTH="92%" BORDER="0" STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" ALIGN="center">

<P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:8pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">Availability&nbsp;for</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:8pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">Last Fiscal</P>

</TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Secured Revolving Line of Credit may be optionally prepaid or terminated, and unutilized commitments may be reduced at any
time, in each case without premium or penalty. In connection with the Secured Revolving Line of Credit, the Borrowers will pay an unused line fee equal to 0.375%&nbsp;per annum, payable monthly on the unused amount of the commitments under the
Secured Revolving Line of Credit. The unused line fee decreases to 0.25%&nbsp;per annum when 35% or more of the Secured Revolving Line of Credit is utilized. The Borrowers will pay (i)&nbsp;a monthly fee on all letters of credit outstanding under
the Secured Revolving Line of Credit equal to the applicable LIBOR margin and (ii)&nbsp;a fronting fee to the Agent equal to 0.125% of all such letters of credit, payable monthly in arrears. </P>

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Amended and Restated Loan Agreement contains covenants that place certain restrictions on the Loan
Parties&#146; ability to, among other things, allow two of the Company&#146;s subsidiaries that manufacture or process inventory for the Loan Parties to borrow secured debt or unsecured debt beyond a certain amount, amend or modify certain terms of
any debt of $50 million or more or subordinated debt, create or suffer to exist any liens upon accounts or inventory, sell or transfer any of Loan Parties&#146; accounts or inventory other than certain ordinary-course transfers, make certain changes
to any Loan Party&#146;s name or form or state of organization without notifying the Agent, liquidate, dissolve, merge, amalgamate, combine or consolidate, or become a party to certain agreements restricting the Loan Parties&#146; ability to incur
or repay debt, grant liens, make distributions, or modify loan agreements. Further restrictions apply when certain payment conditions (the &#147;Payment Conditions&#148;) are not satisfied with respect to specified transactions, events or payments.
The Payment Conditions include that (i)&nbsp;no default or event of default exists and (ii)&nbsp;at all times during the 45 consecutive days immediately prior to such transaction, event or payment and on a pro forma basis after giving effect to such
transaction, event or payment and any incurrence or repayment of indebtedness in connection therewith, the Loan Parties&#146; excess available cash is greater than the greater of 20% of the total commitment amount and $100 million. Such restrictions
limit the Loan Parties&#146; ability to, among other things, create any liens upon any of the Loan Parties&#146; property (other than customary permitted liens and liens on up to $1.5&nbsp;billion of secured credit facilities debt (which amount
includes the Secured Revolving Line of Credit)), declare or make any distributions, create any encumbrance on the ability of a subsidiary to make any upstream payments, make asset dispositions other than certain ordinary course dispositions, make
certain loans, make payments with respect to subordinated debt or certain borrowed money prior to its due date or become a party to certain agreements restricting the Loan Parties&#146; ability to enter into any non arm&#146;s-length transaction
with an affiliate. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Loan Parties are required to repurchase, redeem, defease, repay, create a segregated account for the repayment of, or request
Agent reserve a sufficient available amount under the Secured Revolving Line of Credit for the repayment of, all debt for borrowed money exceeding $50&nbsp;million, by no later than 60 days prior to its maturity date (not including the Secured
Revolving Line of Credit). Any reserved funds for this purpose would not be included in domestic cash calculations. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">If at any time the Loan Parties&#146;
excess available cash is less than the greater of 15% of the total commitment amount and $75 million, the Loan Parties must maintain a minimum fixed charge coverage ratio of 1.00 to 1.00 until (i)&nbsp;no event of default exists and (ii)&nbsp;the
Loan Parties&#146; excess available cash is greater than the greater of 15% of the total commitment amount and $75 million for 45 consecutive days. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The
events of default under the Amended and Restated Loan Agreement include, among other things, payment defaults, the inaccuracy of representations or warranties, defaults in the performance of affirmative and negative covenants, bankruptcy and
insolvency related defaults, a cross-default related to indebtedness in an aggregate amount in excess of $50 million, judgments entered against a Loan Party in an amount that exceeds cumulatively $50 million, certain ERISA events and events related
to Canadian defined benefits plans and a change of control. When a Payment Condition has not been satisfied, additional events of default include, among other things, a loss, theft damage or destruction with respect to any collateral if the amount
not covered by insurance exceeds $50 million. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The preceding description of the Amended and Restated Loan Agreement and the Secured Revolving Line of
Credit is qualified in its entirety by reference to the entire text of the Amended and Restated Loan Agreement, filed as Exhibit 1.01 to this Current Report on Form 8-K and incorporated herein by reference. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>Fifth Amendment to the Wafer Supply Agreement</U> </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">On
April 16, 2015, the Company entered into a fifth amendment (the &#147;Fifth Amendment&#148;) to the Wafer Supply Agreement with GLOBALFOUNDRIES Inc. (&#147;GF&#148;). The primary effect of the Fifth Amendment was to establish volume purchase
commitments and fixed pricing for the 2015 calendar year as well as to modify certain other terms of the Wafer Supply Agreement applicable to wafers for some of AMD&#146;s microprocessor unit, graphics processor unit and semi-custom products to be
delivered by GF to AMD during the 2015 calendar year. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">AMD currently estimates that it will purchase wafers from GF for approximately $1&nbsp;billion in
2015. AMD expects that its future purchases from GF will continue to be material under the Wafer Supply Agreement. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The foregoing description does not
purport to be complete and is qualified in its entirety by reference to the Fifth Amendment, a copy of which will be filed with a Quarterly Report on Form 10-Q. </P>

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Item&nbsp;2.02 Results of Operations and Financial Condition. </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Item&nbsp;7.01 Regulation FD Disclosure. </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The information
in this report furnished pursuant to Items 2.02 and 7.01, including Exhibits 99.1 and 99.2 attached hereto, shall not be deemed &#147;filed&#148; for the purposes of Section&nbsp;18 of the Securities and Exchange Act of 1934, as amended (the
&#147;Exchange Act&#148;), or otherwise subject to the liabilities of that section. It may only be incorporated by reference in another filing under the Exchange Act or the Securities Act of 1933, as amended, if such subsequent filing specifically
references the information furnished pursuant to Items 2.02 and 7.01 of this report. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">On April&nbsp;16, 2015, the Company announced its financial position
and results of operations as of and for its fiscal quarter ended March&nbsp;28, 2015 in an earnings press release that is attached hereto as Exhibit 99.1. Attached hereto as Exhibit 99.2 is financial information and commentary by Devinder Kumar,
Senior Vice President and Chief Financial Officer of the Company, regarding the Company&#146;s fiscal quarter ended March&nbsp;28, 2015. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">To supplement
the Company&#146;s financial results presented on a U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (&#147;GAAP&#148;) basis, the Company&#146;s earnings press release and CFO commentary contain non-GAAP financial measures, including non-GAAP gross
margin, non-GAAP operating expenses, non-GAAP operating income (loss), non-GAAP net income (loss), non-GAAP earnings (loss) per share, Adjusted EBITDA and non-GAAP free cash flow. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Specifically, these non-GAAP financial measures reflect adjustments based on the following: </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Goodwill Impairment</U>: represents impairment charges related to the analysis the Company performed during its annual impairment testing. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Restructuring and other special charges, net</U>: represents charges related to exiting the dense server systems business, including impairment of acquired
intangible assets, employee severance costs, asset impairments, inventory write-down and contract termination charges. In addition, it represents employee severance costs, asset impairments, facility charges and contract termination charges
associated with our restructuring plans and executive officer separation charges. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Lower-of-cost-or-market inventory adjustment</U>: represents a
write-down of the Company&#146;s inventory to its lower of cost or market value. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Amortization of acquired intangible assets</U>: represents
amortization expenses of acquired intangible assets in connection with the Company&#146;s acquisitions of SeaMicro, Inc. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Workforce rebalancing
severance charges</U>: represents employee severance costs associated with a reduction of the Company&#146;s global workforce as part of the ongoing transformation and diversification strategy. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Loss on debt redemption</U>: represents losses that the Company incurred as a result of the repurchase of certain outstanding indebtedness in excess of the
carrying amount of the debt. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Stock-based compensation:</U> represents expense related to employee stock options, restricted stock units and
performance-based restricted stock units under the Company&#146;s stock-based incentive compensation plan. </P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B><U>AMENDED AND RESTATED LOAN AND SECURITY AGREEMENT </U></B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>THIS AMENDED AND RESTATED LOAN AND SECURITY AGREEMENT</B> (this &#147;<U>Agreement</U>&#148;) is dated as of April&nbsp;14, 2015, among
<B>ADVANCED MICRO DEVICES, INC.</B>, a Delaware corporation (&#147;<U>Parent</U>&#148;), <B>AMD INTERNATIONAL SALES&nbsp;&amp; SERVICE, LTD.</B>, a Delaware corporation (&#147;<U>AMDISS</U>&#148;; together with Parent, individually, a
&#147;<U>Borrower</U>&#148; and, collectively, the &#147;<U>Borrowers</U>&#148;), <B>ATI TECHNOLOGIES ULC</B>, an Alberta unlimited liability corporation (the &#147;<U>Canadian Guarantor</U>&#148;), the financial institutions party to this Agreement
from time to time as lenders (collectively, &#147;<U>Lenders</U>&#148;), and <B>BANK OF AMERICA, N.A.,</B> a national banking association, as agent for the Lenders (&#147;<U>Agent</U>&#148;). <B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:24pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B><U>R E C I T A L S: </U></B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Borrowers, the lenders party thereto, and Agent entered into that certain Loan and Security Agreement dated as of November&nbsp;12, 2013, as
amended by that certain First Amendment to Loan and Security Agreement dated as of December&nbsp;11, 2014 (as amended prior to the date hereof, the &#147;<U>Existing Loan Agreement</U>&#148;) pursuant to which the lenders party thereto have made
available certain credit extensions pursuant to the terms and conditions of the Existing Loan Agreement; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Borrowers have requested, and
Agent and Lenders party hereto have agreed, subject to the terms hereof, that the Existing Loan Agreement be amended and restated in order to make certain amendments and modifications to the Existing Loan Agreement; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">This Agreement amends and restates in its entirety the Existing Loan Agreement, and it is the intent of the parties that this Agreement does
not and shall not constitute a novation of the obligations and liabilities of the parties under the Existing Loan Agreement; and </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">It is
further the intent of the parties to confirm that all Obligations (as defined in the Existing Loan Agreement) under the Loan Documents (as defined in the Existing Loan Agreement) shall, other than as specifically provided in this Agreement, continue
in full force and effect. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Borrowers have requested that Lenders continue to provide a credit facility to Borrowers to finance their
mutual and collective business enterprise. Lenders are willing to provide the credit facility on the terms and conditions set forth in this Agreement. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>NOW, THEREFORE</B>, for valuable consideration hereby acknowledged, the parties agree as follows:<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>SECTION 1. DEFINITIONS; RULES OF CONSTRUCTION </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>1.1. <U>Definitions</U>.</B> As used herein, the following terms have the meanings set forth below: </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Account</U>&#148;: (a)&nbsp;all rights to payment with respect to Inventory, whether such rights arise out of the sale, lease,
license, assignment, or other disposition of such Inventory, or the rendition of services with respect thereto, and whether such rights to payment constitute &#147;Accounts&#148; (as defined in the UCC or PPSA), payment intangibles, Letter-of-Credit
Rights, or any other classification of property, or are evidenced in whole or in part by Instruments, Chattel Paper, Documents of Title, or Documents, and (b)&nbsp;all payment intangibles or other rights to payment of a monetary obligation which are
owed to an Obligor by a credit card processor or credit card issuer and which arise out of the use of a credit, debit, or charge card or information contained on or for use with any such card, in each case, for payment for Inventory or the rendition
of services with respect thereto. </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-1- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Account Debtor</U>&#148;: a Person obligated under an Account or Chattel Paper. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Accounts Formula Amount</U>&#148;: (a)&nbsp;with respect to Eligible Accounts owing by Apple Inc. or its Affiliates, Sony Corporation
or its Affiliates, or Microsoft Corporation or its Affiliates, 90% of the Value of Eligible Accounts owing by such Persons, <U>provided</U> that (i)&nbsp;if the Dilution Percent (OEM-Microsoft), Dilution Percent (OEM-Sony), or Dilution Percent
(OEM-Apple), as applicable, calculated with respect to any such Person and its Affiliates is greater than 2% or (ii)&nbsp;if such Person does not have an Investment Grade Rating, then the percentage of Eligible Accounts included in the Accounts
Formula Amount with respect to such Person and its Affiliates shall be determined pursuant to the following clause (b), and (b)&nbsp;with respect to Eligible Accounts owing by any other Account Debtor, 85% of the Value of Eligible Accounts owing by
such Persons. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Acquisition</U>&#148;: a transaction or series of transactions resulting in (a)&nbsp;acquisition of a business,
division or substantially all assets of a Person; (b)&nbsp;record or beneficial ownership of more than 50% of the Equity Interests of a Person; or (c)&nbsp;merger, consolidation or combination of a Borrower or Subsidiary with another Person. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Adjusted EBITDA</U>&#148;: determined on a consolidated basis for Parent and Subsidiaries, net income for the relevant period,
calculated before giving effect to the following, to the extent included in determining net income: (a)&nbsp;interest expense, (b)&nbsp;provision for income taxes, (c)&nbsp;depreciation and amortization expense, (d)&nbsp;gains or losses arising from
the sale of capital assets, (e)&nbsp;gains arising from the write-up of assets, (f)&nbsp;any extraordinary gains (<U>provided</U>, that, an extraordinary gain shall not be excluded from Adjusted EBITDA if such extraordinary gain is directly related
to an extraordinary loss that was recognized in the calculation of net income during the most recently completed four Fiscal Quarters, but only to the extent of such extraordinary loss), (g)&nbsp;non-cash charges reducing net income (excluding
accrued expenses and any losses from the write-down of accounts or inventory), <U>provided</U> that if any such non-cash charges represent an accrual or reserve for potential cash items in any future period, the cash payment in respect thereof in
such future period shall be subtracted from Adjusted EBITDA to such extent, (h)&nbsp;cash severance costs incurred in the Fiscal Quarter ending on or about December&nbsp;27, 2014, or any Fiscal Quarter thereafter, (i)&nbsp;amounts associated with
restructuring, sale, integration, reorganization, or other similar activities in connection with the sale of Real Estate or moving leased locations, in each case accounted for in the Fiscal Quarter ending on or about December&nbsp;27, 2014, or any
Fiscal Quarter thereafter, and (j)&nbsp;cash expenses incurred in connection with Asset Dispositions outside of the ordinary course of business and not prohibited by this Agreement. Anything in this definition to the contrary notwithstanding, any
income or loss from the early extinguishment or modification of Borrowed Money shall be excluded in the determination of net income. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Affiliate</U>&#148;: with respect to a specified Person, another Person that directly, or indirectly through one or more
intermediaries, Controls or is Controlled by or is under common Control with the Person specified. &#147;<U>Control</U>&#148; means the possession, directly or indirectly, of the power to direct or cause the direction of the management or policies
of a Person, whether through the ability to exercise voting power, by contract or otherwise. &#147;<U>Controlling</U>&#148; and &#147;<U>Controlled</U>&#148; have correlative meanings. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Agent Booked GL Reserve</U>&#148;: a reserve established by Agent in its Permitted Discretion based on the product of (a)&nbsp;the
Borrower Booked GL Reserve <U>multiplied by</U> (b)&nbsp;the applicable Accounts Formula Amount. </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-2- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Agent Indemnitees</U>&#148;: Agent and its officers, directors, employees, Affiliates,
agents, attorneys, advisors and other representatives. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Agent Professionals</U>&#148;: attorneys, accountants, appraisers,
auditors, business valuation experts, environmental engineers or consultants, turnaround consultants, and other professionals and experts retained by Agent. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Agreement Currency</U>&#148;: as defined in <B>Section&nbsp;1.5. </B> </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Allocable Amount</U>&#148;: as defined in <B>Section&nbsp;5.11.3</B>. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Anti-Corruption Law</U>&#148;: any law, rule, and regulation of any jurisdiction applicable to any Obligor or its Subsidiaries from
time to time concerning or relating to bribery or corruption. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Anti-Terrorism Law</U>&#148;: any law relating to terrorism or
money laundering, including the Patriot Act and the Proceeds of Crime Act. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Applicable Law</U>&#148;: all laws, rules,
regulations and governmental guidelines applicable to the Person, conduct, transaction, agreement or matter in question, including all applicable statutory law, common law and equitable principles, and all provisions of constitutions, treaties,
statutes, rules, regulations, orders and decrees of Governmental Authorities. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Applicable Margin</U>&#148;: (a)&nbsp;if Average
Availability for the most recently ended Fiscal Month is greater than or equal to 66.66% of the Revolver Commitment as determined on the first day of each Fiscal Month and the Fixed Charge Coverage Ratio for the four Fiscal Quarters most recently
ended is greater than or equal to 1.25 to 1.00, as set forth in the Compliance Certificate most recently delivered to Agent pursuant to <B>Section&nbsp;10.1.2(c)</B>, 0.25%&nbsp;per annum for Base Rate Revolver Loans and 1.25%&nbsp;per annum for
LIBOR Revolver Loans or (b)&nbsp;otherwise, the margin set forth below, based on Average Availability for the most recently ended Fiscal Month as determined on the first day of each Fiscal Month: </P>

</TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Approved Fund</U>&#148;: any Person (other than a natural Person) engaged in making, purchasing,
holding or otherwise investing in commercial loans in its ordinary course of activities, and is administered or managed by a Lender, an entity that administers or manages a Lender, or an Affiliate of either. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Asset Disposition</U>&#148;: a sale, lease (as lessor), exclusive license, consignment, transfer or other disposition of Property of
an Obligor, including a disposition of Property in connection with a sale-leaseback transaction or synthetic lease. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Assignment
and Acceptance</U>&#148;: an assignment agreement between a Lender and Eligible Assignee, in the form of <B>Exhibit A</B> or otherwise satisfactory to Agent. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Availability</U>&#148;: the Borrowing Base <U>minus</U> Revolver Usage. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Availability Reserve</U>&#148;: the sum (without duplication) of (a)&nbsp;the Bank Product Reserve; (b)&nbsp;the aggregate amount of
liabilities secured by Liens upon Collateral that are senior to Agent&#146;s Liens (but imposition of any such reserve shall not waive an Event of Default arising therefrom); (c)&nbsp;the Dilution/GL Reserve; (d)&nbsp;Material Debt Payment Reserves;
(e)&nbsp;reserves for Prior Claims; and (f)&nbsp;such additional reserves, in such amounts and with respect to such matters, as Agent in its Permitted Discretion may elect to impose from time to time. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Average Availability</U>&#148;: for any period, the sum of Availability for each day of such period, <U>divided</U> by the number of
days in such period. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Bank of America</U>&#148;: Bank of America, N.A., a national banking association, and its successors and
assigns. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Bank of America Indemnitees</U>&#148;: Bank of America and its officers, directors, employees, Affiliates, agents,
attorneys, advisors and other representatives. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Bank Product</U>&#148;: any of the following products, services or facilities
extended to any Obligor or Affiliate of an Obligor by a Lender or any of its Affiliates: (a)&nbsp;Cash Management Services; (b)&nbsp;products </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-4- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">
under Hedging Agreements; (c)&nbsp;commercial credit card and merchant card services; (d)&nbsp;supply chain finance arrangements; and (e)&nbsp;leases and other banking products or services;
<U>provided</U>, <U>however</U>, that Letters of Credit and other letters of credit (including, without limitation, Specified Sale/Leaseback LCs) shall not constitute Bank Products for any purposes under the Loan Documents. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Bank Product Debt</U>&#148;: Debt and other obligations of an Obligor or Subsidiary relating to Bank Products. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Bank Product Reserve</U>&#148;: with respect to a Designated Secured Bank Product Obligation, the reserve therefor established by
Agent from time to time at the request of a Secured Bank Product Provider, calculated in a manner (a)&nbsp;determined by Agent with respect to Designated Secured Bank Product Obligations with respect to Bank Products provided by Agent and
(b)&nbsp;in all other cases determined by Agent based on the methodology reasonably developed by such Secured Bank Product Provider to be used in calculating such reserve upon 5 Business Days&#146; written notice provided to Agent and Borrower
Agent. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Bankruptcy Code</U>&#148;: Title 11 of the United States Code. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Base Rate</U>&#148;: for any day, a per annum rate equal to the greatest of (a)&nbsp;the Prime Rate for such day; (b)&nbsp;the
Federal Funds Rate for such day, plus 0.50%; and (c)&nbsp;LIBOR for a 1 month interest period as of such day, plus 1.0%. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Base
Rate Loan</U>&#148;: any Loan that bears interest based on the Base Rate. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Base Rate Revolver Loan</U>&#148;: a Revolver Loan
that bears interest based on the Base Rate. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>BIA</U>&#148;: the Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act (Canada), as amended. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Board of Governors</U>&#148;: the Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrower Booked GL Reserve</U>&#148;: the reserve that is included on the books and records of the Obligors with respect to certain
dilutive items with respect to Accounts, including, without limitation, reserves based on future performance-based rebates, product returns, pricing protection credits, marketing development funds and other rebates, all of which are liabilities that
are deemed occurring under GAAP at the point of sale. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrowed Money</U>&#148;: with respect to any Person, without duplication,
its (a)&nbsp;Debt that (i)&nbsp;arises from the lending of money by any other Person to such Person, (ii)&nbsp;is evidenced by notes, drafts, bonds, debentures, credit documents or similar instruments, (iii)&nbsp;accrues interest or is a type upon
which interest charges are customarily paid (excluding trade payables owing in the Ordinary Course of Business), or (iv)&nbsp;was issued or assumed as full or partial payment for Property; (b)&nbsp;Capital Leases; (c)&nbsp;reimbursement obligations
with respect to letters of credit; and (d)&nbsp;guaranties of any Debt of the foregoing types owing by another Person. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrower
Agent</U>&#148;: as defined in <B>Section&nbsp;4.4</B>. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrower Materials</U>&#148;: Borrowing Base Certificates, Compliance
Certificates and other information, reports, financial statements and other materials delivered by an Obligor hereunder, as well as other Reports and information provided by Agent to Lenders. </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-5- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrowing</U>&#148;: a group of Loans that are made or converted together on the same
day and have the same interest option and, if applicable, Interest Period. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrowing Base</U>&#148;: on any date of
determination, an amount equal to the lesser of (a)&nbsp;the aggregate Revolver Commitments; and (b)&nbsp;the sum of the Accounts Formula Amount <U>minus</U> the Availability Reserve. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrowing Base Parties</U>&#148;: the Borrowers and the Canadian Guarantor. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrowing Base Certificate</U>&#148;: a certificate, in form and substance satisfactory to Agent, by which Borrower Agent certifies
the Borrowing Base. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Borrowing Base Reporting Date</U>&#148;: (a)&nbsp;during any period that is not an Increased Reporting
Period, the 20th calendar day after each of (i)&nbsp;the end of each Fiscal Quarter of the Parent and (ii)&nbsp;the end of each Fiscal Month of Parent in which Revolver Usage was at any time $25,000,000 or more for three consecutive Business Days
ending at any time during such Fiscal Month, and the end of the Fiscal Month immediately thereafter, and (b)&nbsp;during any Increased Reporting Period, (i)&nbsp;with respect to Borrowing Base Certificates, every other Thursday (i.e., bi-weekly),
commencing on Thursday of the week immediately following the week in which the Increased Reporting Period begins and (ii)&nbsp;with respect to deliverables under <B>Section&nbsp;8.2.1</B>, Thursday of each week, commencing on Thursday of the week
immediately following the week in which the Increased Reporting Period begins. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Business Day</U>&#148;: any day other than a
Saturday, Sunday or other day on which commercial banks are authorized to close under the laws of, or are in fact closed in, North Carolina and California, and if such day relates to a LIBOR Loan, any such day on which dealings in Dollar deposits
are conducted between banks in the London interbank Eurodollar market. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Canada</U>&#148;: the country of Canada and any province
or territory thereof. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Canadian Benefit Plan</U>&#148;: any material plan, fund, program, or policy, whether oral or written,
formal or informal, funded or unfunded, insured or uninsured, providing employee benefits, including such medical, hospital care, dental, sickness, accident, disability, life insurance, pension, retirement or savings benefits, under which any
Obligor or any Subsidiary has any liability with respect to any employee or former employee, but excluding any Canadian Pension Plans and excluding any stock option or share purchase plan that is an employee benefit plan that is required to be
registered under any applicable Canadian federal, provincial or territorial employee benefit legislation, whether or not registered under any such legislation, which is, or has been, maintained or contributed to by, or to which there is or may be an
obligation to contribute by, an Obligor or Subsidiary operating in Canada in respect of any Person&#146;s employment in Canada with such Obligor or Subsidiary. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Canadian Defined Benefit Plan</U>&#148;: a Canadian Pension Plan, which contains a &#147;defined benefit provision,&#148; as defined
in subsection 147.1(1) of the ITA. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Canadian Guarantor</U>&#148;: has the meaning given such term in the preamble. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Canadian MEPP</U>&#148;: any plan that is a multi-employer pension plan as defined under the applicable pension standards
legislation. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Canadian Pension Plans</U>&#148;: any plan, program or arrangement that is a pension plan that is required to be
registered under any applicable Canadian federal, provincial or territorial pension legislation, whether or not registered under any such legislation, which is, or has been, maintained or </P>

<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Cash Collateral</U>&#148;: cash, and any interest or other income earned thereon, that is delivered to Agent to Cash Collateralize

that would not result in the Cash Dominion Release Date occurring more than twice in any 12 consecutive month period. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Cash

other party to an agreement; and (c)&nbsp;arrangement (i)&nbsp;to purchase any primary obligation or security therefor, (ii)&nbsp;to supply funds for the purchase or payment of any primary obligation, (iii)&nbsp;to maintain or assure working
capital, equity capital, net worth or solvency of the primary obligor, (iv)&nbsp;to purchase Property or services for the purpose of assuring the ability of the primary obligor to perform a primary obligation, or (v)&nbsp;otherwise to assure or hold
harmless the holder of any primary obligation against loss in respect thereof. The amount of any Contingent Obligation shall be deemed to be the stated or determinable amount of the primary obligation (or, if less, the maximum amount for which such
Person may be liable under the instrument evidencing the Contingent Obligation) or, if not stated or determinable, the maximum reasonably anticipated liability with respect thereto. </P>

lender or group of lenders or investors and whether such revision, restructuring or refinancing is under one or more Debt facilities or commercial paper facilities, indentures or other agreements, in each case with banks or other institutional
lenders or trustees or investors providing for revolving credit loans, term loans, notes or letters of credit, together with related documents thereto (including any guaranty agreements and security documents). </P>

<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Default</U>&#148;: an event or condition that, with the lapse of time or giving of notice, would constitute an Event of Default. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Default Rate</U>&#148;: for any Obligation (including, to the extent permitted by law, interest not paid when due), 2% plus the
interest rate otherwise applicable thereto. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Defaulting Lender</U>&#148;: any Lender that (a)&nbsp;has failed to comply with its
funding obligations hereunder, and such failure is not cured within two Business Days; (b)&nbsp;has notified Agent or any Borrower that such Lender does not intend to comply with its funding obligations hereunder or under any other credit facility,
or has made a public statement to that effect; (c)&nbsp;has failed, within three Business Days following request by Agent or any Borrower, to confirm in a manner satisfactory to Agent and Borrowers that such Lender will comply with its funding
obligations hereunder (provided that such Lender shall cease to be a Defaulting Lender pursuant to this clause (c)&nbsp;upon receipt of such written confirmation by the Agent and the Borrower Agent); or (d)&nbsp;has, or has a direct or indirect
parent company that has, become the subject of an Insolvency Proceeding (including reorganization, liquidation, or appointment of a receiver, custodian, administrator or similar Person by the Federal Deposit Insurance Corporation or any other
regulatory authority); <U>provided</U>, <U>however</U>, that a Lender shall not be a Defaulting Lender solely by virtue of a Governmental Authority&#146;s ownership of an equity interest in such Lender or parent company unless the ownership provides
immunity for such Lender from jurisdiction of courts within the United States or from enforcement of judgments or writs of attachment on its assets, or permits such Lender or Governmental Authority to repudiate or otherwise to reject such
Lender&#146;s agreements; and </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-10- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">
<U>provided</U> <U>further</U>, that a Lender shall not be deemed to be a Defaulting Lender under clauses (a), (b)&nbsp;or (c)&nbsp;if it has notified Agent and Borrowers in writing that it will
not make a funding because a condition to funding (specifically identified in the notice) is not or cannot be satisfied. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Deposit
Account</U>&#148;: as defined in the UCC or PPSA and including any bank account with a depository function. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Designated Secured
Bank Product Obligation</U>&#148;: a Secured Bank Product Obligation that has been designated by a Secured Bank Product Provider to Agent as being a &#147;Designated Secured Bank Product Obligation&#148; pursuant to at least 5 Business Days&#146;
written notice to Agent and Borrower Agent which notice includes a methodology to be used in calculating a Bank Product Reserve for such Secured Bank Product. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Designated Jurisdiction</U>&#148;: any country or territory that is the subject of any Sanction. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution/GL Reserve</U>&#148;: either, in the Agent&#146;s Permitted Discretion, (a)&nbsp;the Agent Booked GL Reserve or (b)&nbsp;sum
of (i)&nbsp;the Dilution Reserve (OEM) <U>plus</U> (ii)&nbsp;the Dilution Reserve (OEM-Microsoft) <U>plus</U> (iii)&nbsp;the Dilution Reserve (OEM-Sony) <U>plus</U> (iv)&nbsp;the Dilution Reserve (OEM-Apple) <U>plus</U> (v)&nbsp;the Dilution Reserve
(Distributors). </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Percent (Distributors)</U>&#148;: the greater of (a)&nbsp;the percent, determined for Borrowing Base
Parties&#146; most recently completed three Fiscal Months, equal to (i)&nbsp;bad debt write-downs or write-offs, discounts, returns, promotions, credits, credit memos and other dilutive items with respect to Accounts owing by Distributors during
such period, <U>divided</U> <U>by</U> (ii)&nbsp;gross sales to Distributors during such period, and (b)&nbsp;the percent, determined for the Borrowing Base Parties&#146; most recently completed twelve Fiscal Months, equal to (i)&nbsp;bad debt
write-downs or write-offs, discounts, returns, promotions, credits, credit memos and other dilutive items with respect to Accounts owing by Distributors during such period, <U>divided</U> <U>by</U> (ii)&nbsp;gross sales to Distributors during such
period. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Percent (OEM)</U>&#148;: the greater of (a)&nbsp;the percent, determined for Borrowing Base Parties&#146; most
recently completed three Fiscal Months, equal to (i)&nbsp;bad debt write-downs or write-offs, discounts, returns, promotions, credits, credit memos and other dilutive items with respect to Accounts owing by OEMs (other than Specified OEMs) during
such period, <U>divided</U> <U>by</U> (ii)&nbsp;gross sales to OEMs (other than Specified OEMs) during such period, and (b)&nbsp;the percent, determined for the Borrowing Base Parties&#146; most recently completed twelve Fiscal Months, equal to
(i)&nbsp;bad debt write-downs or write-offs, discounts, returns, promotions, credits, credit memos and other dilutive items with respect to Accounts owing by OEMs (other than Specified OEMs) during such period, <U>divided</U> <U>by</U>
(ii)&nbsp;gross sales to OEMs (other than Specified OEMs) during such period. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Percent (OEM-Apple)</U>&#148;: the
greater of (a)&nbsp;the percent, determined for Borrowing Base Parties&#146; most recently completed three Fiscal Months, equal to (i)&nbsp;bad debt write-downs or write-offs, discounts, returns, promotions, credits, credit memos and other dilutive
items with respect to Accounts owing by Apple Inc. and its Affiliates during such period, <U>divided</U> <U>by</U> (ii)&nbsp;gross sales to Apple Inc. and its Affiliates during such period, and (b)&nbsp;the percent, determined for the Borrowing Base
Parties&#146; most recently completed twelve Fiscal Months, equal to (i)&nbsp;bad debt write-downs or write-offs, discounts, returns, promotions, credits, credit memos and other dilutive items with respect to Accounts owing by Apple Inc. and its
Affiliates during such period, <U>divided</U> <U>by</U> (ii)&nbsp;gross sales to Apple Inc. and its Affiliates during such period. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Percent (OEM-Microsoft)</U>&#148;: the greater of (a)&nbsp;the percent, determined for Borrowing Base Parties&#146; most
recently completed three Fiscal Months, equal to (i)&nbsp;bad debt write-downs or write-</P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-11- </P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">

<U>by</U> (ii)&nbsp;gross sales to Microsoft Corporation and its Affiliates during such period, and (b)&nbsp;the percent, determined for the Borrowing Base Parties&#146; most recently completed twelve Fiscal Months, equal to (i)&nbsp;bad debt
write-downs or write-offs, discounts, returns, promotions, credits, credit memos and other dilutive items with respect to Accounts owing by Microsoft Corporation or its Affiliates during such period, <U>divided</U> <U>by</U> (ii)&nbsp;gross sales to
Microsoft Corporation and its Affiliates during such period. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Percent (OEM-Sony)</U>&#148;: the greater of (a)&nbsp;the

<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Reserve (Distributors)</U>&#148;: as of any date of determination, a reserve equal to the product of (a)&nbsp;1.0% for each
whole percentage point (or portion thereof) by which the Dilution Percent (Distributors) exceeds five percent (5.0%)&nbsp;<U>times</U> (b)&nbsp;the Borrowing Base Parties&#146; outstanding Eligible Accounts owed by Distributors. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Reserve (OEM)</U>&#148;: as of any date of determination, a reserve equal to the product of (a)&nbsp;1.0% for each whole
percentage point (or portion thereof) by which the Dilution Percent (OEM) exceeds five percent (5.0%)&nbsp;<U>times</U> (b)&nbsp;the Borrowing Base Parties&#146; outstanding Eligible Accounts owed by OEMs (other than Specified OEMs). </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Reserve (OEM-Apple)</U>&#148;: as of any date of determination, a reserve equal to the product of (a)&nbsp;1.0% for each
whole percentage point (or portion thereof) by which the Dilution Percent (OEM-Apple) exceeds five percent (5.0%)&nbsp;<U>times</U> (b)&nbsp;the Borrowing Base Parties&#146; outstanding Eligible Accounts owed by Apple Inc. or its Affiliates. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dilution Reserve (OEM-Microsoft)</U>&#148;: as of any date of determination, a reserve equal to the product of (a)&nbsp;1.0% for each
whole percentage point (or portion thereof) by which the Dilution Percent (OEM-Microsoft) exceeds five percent (5.0%)&nbsp;<U>times</U> (b)&nbsp;the Borrowing Base Parties&#146; outstanding Eligible Accounts owed by Microsoft Corporation or its

Corporation or its Affiliates. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Distribution</U>&#148;: any declaration or payment of a distribution, interest or dividend on any
Equity Interest (other than payment-in-kind); distribution, advance or repayment of Debt to a holder of Equity Interests; or purchase, redemption, or other acquisition or retirement for value of any Equity Interest. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Distributor</U>&#148;: any Account Debtor described or coded as a &#147;Distributor&#148; (i.e., with an &#147;O2&#148; designation
or other designation approved by Agent) as described in Obligors&#146; Accounts aging details. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dollars</U>&#148;: lawful money
of the United States. </P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B></B>&#147;<U>Domestic Cash</U>&#148;: as of any date of determination, the available balance
amount (and not the ledger balance amount) on such date of all Dollar-denominated unrestricted cash and Cash Equivalents of the Borrowing Base Parties that are, on such date, in Deposit Accounts or Securities Accounts (other than, in each case, any
Specified Escrow Account) located in the United States and with respect to which a Borrowing Base Party is the sole account holder, so long as (a)&nbsp;such cash, Cash Equivalents, Deposit Accounts, and Securities Accounts are not subject to any
Lien other than the Agent&#146;s Lien (to the extent required in the Loan Documents), or as otherwise permitted under <B>Section&nbsp;10.2.2(ix)</B>, (b)&nbsp;with respect to any particular Deposit Account or Securities Account, the Borrowing Base
Parties have free and unfettered access and withdrawal rights with respect to such Deposit Account and Securities Account, and (c)&nbsp;with respect to any particular Deposit Account or Securities Account, Agent has the ability on any given day,
during normal business hours with respect to the bank or other financial institution that maintains such Deposit Account or Securities Account, to view the balances in such Deposit Account or Securities Account or such other arrangements acceptable
to Agent have been made for Agent to obtain such information. <B> </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Dominion Account</U>&#148;: a special account established
by the Borrowing Base Parties at Bank of America to which the funds in the Subject Accounts are transferred and concentrated on a regular basis and, during a Cash Dominion period, over which Agent has exclusive control for withdrawal purposes. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Eligible Account</U>&#148;: an Account owing to a Borrowing Base Party by an OEM or Distributor that arises in the Ordinary Course of
Business from the sale of Inventory, is payable in Dollars, and is deemed by Agent, in its Permitted Discretion, to be an Eligible Account. Without limiting the foregoing, no Account shall be an Eligible Account if </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(a) it is unpaid for more than 60 days (or 90 days in the case of Accounts owing by Hewlett-Packard or its Subsidiaries) after the original
due date, or more than 120 days after the original invoice date; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(b) 50% or more of the Accounts owing by the Account Debtor are not
Eligible Accounts; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(c) (i)&nbsp;with respect to Accounts owed by Hewlett-Packard or its Subsidiaries, such Account when aggregated with
other Accounts owing by Hewlett-Packard or its Affiliates exceeds 30% of the Total Accounts (or such higher percentage as Agent may establish for Hewlett-Packard and its Subsidiaries from time to time), (ii)&nbsp;with respect to Accounts owed by
Lenovo Group Limited or its Subsidiaries, such Account when aggregated with other Accounts owing by Lenovo Group Limited or its Affiliates exceeds 30% of the Total Accounts (or such higher percentage as Agent may establish for Lenovo Group Limited
and its Subsidiaries from time to time), (iii)&nbsp;with respect to Accounts owed by Sony Corporation or its Subsidiaries, such Account when aggregated with other Accounts owing by Sony Corporation or its Affiliates exceeds 20% of the Total Accounts
(or such higher percentage as Agent may establish for Sony Corporation and its Subsidiaries from time to time), (iv)&nbsp;with respect to Accounts owed by Microsoft Corporation or its Subsidiaries, such Account when aggregated with other Accounts
owing by Microsoft Corporation or its Affiliates exceeds 25% of the Total Accounts (or such higher percentage as Agent may establish for Microsoft Corporation and its Subsidiaries from time to time), (v)&nbsp;with respect to Accounts owed by any
other Account Debtor or its Subsidiaries that, as of the date of the most recent Borrowing Base Certificate delivered to the Agent, has an Investment Grade Rating, such Account when aggregated with other Accounts owing by such Account Debtor or its
Affiliates exceeds 20% of the Total Accounts (or such higher percentage as Agent may establish for such Account Debtor and its Subsidiaries from time to time), and (vi)&nbsp;with respect to Accounts owed by any other Account Debtor, such Account
when aggregated with other Accounts owing by such Account Debtor or its Affiliates exceeds 15% of the Total Accounts (or such higher percentage as Agent may establish for the Account Debtor from time to time); provided that Agent may, in its
Permitted Discretion, reduce the concentration percentages described above based on the Agent&#146;s determination of the applicable Accounts Debtor&#146;s creditworthiness; </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-13- </P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(d) it does not conform with a covenant or representation herein; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(e) it is owing by a creditor or supplier, or is otherwise subject to a potential offset, counterclaim, dispute, deduction, discount,
recoupment, reserve, defense, chargeback, credit or allowance or otherwise constitutes a contra-account (but ineligibility shall be limited to the amount thereof); </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(f) an Insolvency Proceeding has been commenced by or against the Account Debtor; or the Account Debtor has failed, has suspended or ceased
doing business, is liquidating, dissolving or winding up its affairs, is not Solvent, or is subject to Sanctions or any specially designated nationals list maintained by OFAC; or the applicable Borrowing Base Party is not able to bring suit or

shall not exceed 10% of the Borrowing Base (determined without giving effect to this clause (g)); </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(h) with respect to any Account Debtor

that are subject to an Intercreditor Agreement); </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(k) the goods giving rise to it have not been delivered to the Account Debtor, the

<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(o) it represents a progress billing or retainage, or relates to services for which a performance, surety or completion bond or similar
assurance has been issued; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(o) it is owing from a credit card processor or credit card issuer or which arises out of the use of a credit,
debit or charge card, or information contained on or for use with any such card; </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-14- </P>

calculating delinquent portions of Accounts under clauses (a)&nbsp;and (b), credit balances more than 120 days old will be excluded. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Eligible Assignee</U>&#148;: a Person that is (a)&nbsp;a Lender, Affiliate of a Lender or Approved Fund; (b)&nbsp;a financial
institution approved by Borrower Agent (which approval shall not be unreasonably withheld or delayed, and shall be deemed given if no objection is made within two Business Days after notice of the proposed assignment) and Agent (not to be
unreasonably withheld or delayed) that extends revolving credit facilities of this type in its ordinary course of business (it being understood and agreed that during an Event of Default, no consent of the Borrower Agent shall be required); and
(c)&nbsp;during an Event of Default, any Person acceptable to Agent in its discretion (not to be unreasonably withheld or delayed). </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Enforcement Action</U>&#148;: any action to enforce any Obligations (other than Secured Bank Product Obligations) or Loan Documents
or to exercise any rights or remedies relating to any Collateral (whether by judicial action, self-help, notification of Account Debtors, setoff or recoupment, credit bid, action in an Obligor&#146;s Insolvency Proceeding or otherwise). </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Environmental Laws</U>&#148;: Applicable Laws (including programs, permits and guidance promulgated by regulators) relating to public
health (other than occupational safety and health regulated by OSHA) or the protection or pollution of the environment, including CERCLA, RCRA, CWA, and the environmental protection legislation of the provinces and territories of Canada. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Environmental Notice</U>&#148;: a notice (whether written or oral) from any Governmental Authority or other Person of any possible
noncompliance with, investigation of a possible violation of, litigation relating to, or potential fine or liability under any Environmental Law, or with respect to any Environmental Release, environmental pollution or hazardous materials, including
any complaint, summons, citation, order, claim, demand or request for correction, remediation or otherwise. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Environmental
Release</U>&#148;: a release as defined in CERCLA or under any other Environmental Law. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Equity Interest</U>&#148;: the interest
of any (a)&nbsp;shareholder in a corporation; (b)&nbsp;partner in a partnership (whether general, limited, limited liability or joint venture); (c)&nbsp;member in a limited liability company; or (d)&nbsp;other Person having any other form of equity
security or ownership interest; <U>provided</U>, that, for the avoidance of doubt, the parties hereto agree that &#147;<U>Equity Interests</U>&#148; does not include any notes or other instruments issued pursuant to the Indentures or any other
convertible Debt permitted to be incurred under this Agreement. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>ERISA</U>&#148;: the Employee Retirement Income Security Act of

the meaning of Section&nbsp;414(b) or (c)&nbsp;of the Code (and Sections 414(m) and (o)&nbsp;of the Code for purposes of provisions relating to Section&nbsp;412 of the Code). </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-15- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>ERISA Event</U>&#148;: (a)&nbsp;a Reportable Event with respect to a Pension Plan;
(b)&nbsp;a withdrawal by any Obligor or ERISA Affiliate from a Pension Plan subject to Section&nbsp;4063 of ERISA during a plan year in which it was a substantial employer (as defined in Section&nbsp;4001(a)(2) of ERISA) or a cessation of operations
that is treated as such a withdrawal under Section&nbsp;4062(e) of ERISA; (c)&nbsp;a complete or partial withdrawal by any Obligor or ERISA Affiliate from a Multiemployer Plan or notification that a Multiemployer Plan is in reorganization;
(d)&nbsp;the filing of a notice of intent to terminate, the treatment of a Plan amendment as a termination under Section&nbsp;4041 or 4041A of ERISA, or the commencement of proceedings by the PBGC to terminate a Pension Plan or Multiemployer Plan;
(e)&nbsp;the determination that any Pension Plan or Multiemployer Plan is considered an at risk plan or a plan in critical or endangered status under the Code, ERISA or the Pension Protection Act of 2006; (f)&nbsp;an event or condition which
constitutes grounds under Section&nbsp;4042 of ERISA for the termination of, or the appointment of a trustee to administer, any Pension Plan or Multiemployer Plan; or (g)&nbsp;the imposition of any liability under Title IV of ERISA, other than for
PBGC premiums due but not delinquent under Section&nbsp;4007 of ERISA, upon any Obligor or ERISA Affiliate. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Event of

<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Excluded Swap Obligation</U>&#148;: with respect to an Obligor, each Swap Obligation as to which, and only to the extent that, such
Obligor&#146;s guaranty of or grant of a Lien as security for such Swap Obligation is or becomes illegal under the Commodity Exchange Act because the Obligor does not constitute an &#147;eligible contract participant&#148; as defined in the act
(determined after giving effect to any keepwell, support or other agreement for the benefit of such Obligor and all guarantees of Swap Obligations by other Obligors) when such guaranty or grant of Lien becomes effective with respect to the Swap
Obligation. If a Hedging Agreement governs more than one Swap Obligation, only the Swap Obligation(s) or portions thereof described in the foregoing sentence shall be Excluded Swap Obligation(s) for the applicable Obligor. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Excluded Taxes</U>&#148;: (a)&nbsp;Taxes imposed on or measured by a Recipient&#146;s net income (however denominated), franchise

subdivision thereof), or (ii)&nbsp;constituting Other Connection Taxes; (b)&nbsp;U.S. federal withholding Taxes imposed on amounts payable to or for the account of a Lender with respect to its interest in a Loan or Commitment pursuant to a law in
effect when the Lender acquires such interest (except pursuant to an assignment request by Borrower Agent under <B>Section&nbsp;13.4</B>) or changes its Lending Office, unless the Taxes were payable to its assignor immediately prior to such
assignment or to the Lender immediately prior to its change in Lending Office; (c)&nbsp;Taxes attributable to a Recipient&#146;s failure to comply with <B>Section&nbsp;5.10</B>; and (d)&nbsp;U.S. federal withholding Taxes imposed pursuant to FATCA.

published on the applicable Reuters screen page (or other commercially available source designated by Agent from time to time); <U>provided</U>, that any such comparable or successor rate shall be applied by Agent, if administratively feasible, in a
manner consistent with market practice and (b)&nbsp;zero. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>LIBOR Loan</U>&#148;: each set of LIBOR Revolver Loans having a common
length and commencement of Interest Period. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>LIBOR Revolver Loan</U>&#148;: a Revolver Loan that bears interest based on LIBOR.
</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>License</U>&#148;: any license or agreement under which an Obligor is authorized to use Intellectual Property in connection with
any manufacture, marketing, distribution or disposition of Collateral, any use of Property or any other conduct of its business. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Licensor</U>&#148;: any Person from whom an Obligor obtains the right to use any Intellectual Property. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Lien</U>&#148;: a Person&#146;s interest in Property securing an obligation owed to, or a claim by, such Person, including any lien,
security interest, pledge, hypothecation, assignment, trust, reservation, encroachment, easement, right-of-way, covenant, condition, restriction, lease, or other title exception or encumbrance. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Lien Waiver</U>&#148;: an agreement, in form and substance satisfactory to Agent, by which (a)&nbsp;for any material Collateral
located on leased premises, the lessor waives or subordinates any Lien it may have on the Collateral, and agrees to permit Agent to enter upon the premises and remove the Collateral or to use the premises to store or dispose of the Collateral;
(b)&nbsp;for any Collateral held by a warehouseman, processor, shipper, customs broker or freight forwarder, such Person waives or subordinates any Lien it may have on the Collateral; and (c)&nbsp;for any Collateral held by a bailee, such Person
acknowledges Agent&#146;s Lien, waives or subordinates any Lien it may have on the Collateral. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Loan</U>&#148;: a Revolver Loan.
</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Loan Documents</U>&#148;: this Agreement, Other Agreements and Security Documents. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Loan Year</U>&#148;: each 12 month period commencing on the Restatement Effective Date and on each anniversary of the Restatement
Effective Date. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Mandatory Cost</U>&#148;: any amount incurred periodically by a Lender constituting fees, costs or charges
imposed by any Governmental Authority on lenders generally in the jurisdiction where such Lender is domiciled, is subject to regulation or has its office through which it performs its obligations hereunder. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;<U>Margin Stock</U>&#148;: as defined in Regulation U of the Board of Governors. </P>
