 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>SECTION 10. COVENANTS AND CONTINUING AGREEMENTS </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1. <U>Affirmative Covenants</U>.</B> As long as any Commitments or Obligations are outstanding, each Obligor shall:<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.1. <U>Inspections; Appraisals</U></B>.<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(a) Permit Agent from time to time, subject (except when a Default or Event of Default exists) to reasonable notice and normal business hours,
to visit and inspect the Properties of any Obligor, inspect, audit and make extracts from any Obligor&#146;s books and records, and discuss with its officers, employees, agents, advisors and independent accountants such Obligor&#146;s business,
financial condition, assets, prospects and results of operations. Lenders may participate in any such visit or inspection, at their own expense. Neither Agent nor any Lender shall have any duty to any Obligor to make any inspection, nor to share any
results of any inspection, appraisal or report with any Obligor. Obligors acknowledge that all inspections, appraisals and reports are prepared by Agent and Lenders for their purposes, and Obligors shall not be entitled to rely upon them. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(b) Within 10 Business Days (or such longer period as agreed by Agent in its sole discretion) after Agent&#146;s demand, reimburse Agent for
all reasonable and documented charges, costs and expenses of Agent in connection with examinations of any Obligor&#146;s books and records or any other financial or Collateral matters as Agent deems appropriate, up to one time per Loan Year;
<U>provided</U>, <U>however</U>, that (i)&nbsp;if Excess Cash Availability is equal to or less than the Applicable Threshold Amount (Level II) at any time during any Loan Year, the Obligors shall reimburse Agent for such charges, costs and expenses
for one additional examination in such Loan Year and (ii)&nbsp;if an examination is initiated during a Default or Event of Default, all charges, costs and expenses therefor shall be reimbursed by Obligors without regard to such limits. Obligors
agree to pay Agent&#146;s then standard charges for examination activities, including the standard charges of Agent&#146;s internal examination and appraisal groups, as well as the charges of any third party used for such purposes. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.2. <U>Financial and Other Information</U>.</B> Keep adequate records and books of account with respect to its business activities, in
which proper entries are made in accordance with GAAP reflecting all financial transactions; and furnish to Agent and Lenders (the documents required to be delivered pursuant to <B>Sections 10.1.2(a)</B>,<B> (b)</B>&nbsp;and <B>(f)</B>&nbsp;shall be
deemed to have been delivered on the date on which such documents are posted on the Securities and Exchange Commission&#146;s website at <U>www.sec.gov</U> and on the System for Electronic Document Analysis and Retrieval (SEDAR) at
<U>www.sedar.com</U>, provided that, upon request by Agent, Parent shall deliver a copy of such documents to Agent):<B> </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(a) as soon
as available, and in any event within 90 days after the close of each Fiscal Year, balance sheets as of the end of such Fiscal Year and the related statements of income, cash flow and shareholders equity for such Fiscal Year, on a consolidated basis
for Parent and Subsidiaries, which consolidated statements shall be audited and certified (without qualification) by a firm of independent certified public accountants of recognized standing selected by Obligors and acceptable to Agent, and shall
set forth in comparative form corresponding figures for the preceding Fiscal Year and other information acceptable to Agent; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(b) as soon
as available, and in any event within 45 days after the end of each Fiscal Quarter, unaudited balance sheets as of the end of such Fiscal Quarter and the related statements of income and cash flows for such Fiscal Quarter and for the portion of the
Fiscal Year then elapsed, on consolidated and consolidating bases for Parent and Subsidiaries, setting forth in comparative form corresponding figures for the preceding Fiscal Year and fairly presenting the financial position and results of
operations for such Fiscal Quarter and period, subject to normal year-end adjustments and the absence of footnotes; </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-65- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(c) concurrently with delivery of financial statements under clauses (a)&nbsp;and (b)&nbsp;above,
or more frequently if requested by Agent while a Default or Event of Default exists, a Compliance Certificate executed by a Financial Reporting Officer of Borrower Agent; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(d) not later than 30 days after the end of each Fiscal Year, projections of Parent and Subsidiaries&#146; consolidated balance sheets,
results of operations, cash flow and Availability and Excess Cash Availability for such Fiscal Year, Fiscal Quarter by Fiscal Quarter; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(e) at Agent&#146;s request, a listing of each Obligor&#146;s trade payables, specifying the trade creditor and balance due, and a detailed
trade payable aging, all in form satisfactory to Agent; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(f) promptly after the sending or filing thereof, copies of any proxy statements,
financial statements or reports that Parent has made generally available to its shareholders; copies of any regular, periodic and special reports or registration statements or prospectuses that Parent or any Subsidiary files with the Securities and
Exchange Commission or any Canadian federal or provincial securities commission or any other Governmental Authority, or any securities exchange; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(g) such other reports and information (financial or otherwise) as Agent may request from time to time in connection with any Collateral or
any Obligor&#146;s, Subsidiary&#146;s or other Obligor&#146;s financial condition or business. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.3. <U>Notices</U>.</B> Notify
Agent in writing, promptly after an Obligor&#146;s obtaining knowledge thereof, of any of the following that affects an Obligor: (a)&nbsp;the threat or commencement of any proceeding or investigation, whether or not covered by insurance, if an
adverse determination could reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect; (b)&nbsp;any pending or threatened (in writing) material labor dispute, strike or walkout, or the expiration of any material labor contract; (c)&nbsp;any default
under a Material Contract by any Person party thereto or termination of a Material Contract if such default or termination could reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect; (d)&nbsp;the existence of any Default or Event of Default;
(e)&nbsp;any judgment in an amount exceeding (i)&nbsp;$50,000,000 so long as the Payment Conditions have been satisfied at the time of such judgment and (ii)&nbsp;$5,000,000 if such judgment occurs when the Payment Conditions are not satisfied;
(f)&nbsp;the assertion of any Intellectual Property Claim, if an adverse resolution could reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect; (g)&nbsp;any violation or asserted violation of any Applicable Law (including ERISA, OSHA, FLSA, or
any Environmental Laws), if an adverse resolution could reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect; (h)&nbsp;any Environmental Release by an Obligor or on any Property owned, leased or occupied by an Obligor; or receipt of any
Environmental Notice; (i)&nbsp;the occurrence of any ERISA Event; (j)&nbsp;the discharge of or any withdrawal or resignation by Obligors&#146; independent accountants; (k)&nbsp;(i)&nbsp;any default in, or breach of, a Canadian Defined Benefit Plan,
(ii)&nbsp;any action or inaction of a plan sponsor or administrator that could lead to a Termination Event, (iii)&nbsp;receipt of any notice from, or any action of, any Governmental Authority that that could lead to a Termination Event,
(iv)&nbsp;copies of all actuarial valuations conducted for all Canadian Defined Benefit Plans, and (v)&nbsp;the existence of any Unfunded Pension Liability in any Canadian Defined Benefit Plans; which, for each of clause (i)-(v), results, or could
reasonably be expected to result in, a Material Adverse Effect; or (l)&nbsp;the occurrence of a default or event of default, or receipt of a notice asserting a default or event of default, under the Conditional Grant Agreement.<B> </B></P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.5. <U>Compliance with Laws</U>.</B> Comply with all Applicable Laws, including ERISA,
Environmental Laws, FLSA, OSHA, Anti-Terrorism Laws, and laws regarding collection and payment of Taxes, and maintain all Governmental Approvals necessary to the ownership of its Properties or conduct of its business, unless failure to comply (other
than failure to comply with Anti-Terrorism Laws) or maintain could not reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect. Without limiting the generality of the foregoing, if any Environmental Release occurs at or on any Properties of any
Obligor, it shall act promptly and diligently to investigate and report to Agent and all appropriate Governmental Authorities the extent of, and to make appropriate remedial action to eliminate, such Environmental Release, whether or not directed to
do so by any Governmental Authority. The Obligors will maintain in effect and enforce policies and procedures designed to cause compliance by the Obligors, their respective Subsidiaries and their respective directors, officers, employees and agents
with Anti-Corruption Laws and applicable Sanctions.<B> </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.6. <U>Taxes</U>.</B> Pay and discharge all Taxes prior to the date on
which they become delinquent or penalties attach, unless such Taxes are being Properly Contested. <B> </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.7.
<U>Insurance</U>.</B> In addition to the insurance required hereunder with respect to Collateral, maintain insurance with insurers (with an A.M. Best or S&amp;P rating of at least A-, unless otherwise approved by Agent in its discretion)
satisfactory to Agent, (a)&nbsp;with respect to the Properties and business of Obligors of such type (including product liability, workers&#146; compensation, larceny, embezzlement, or other criminal misappropriation insurance), in such amounts, and
with such coverages and deductibles as are customary for companies similarly situated; and (b)&nbsp;business interruption insurance in an amount consistent with past practices. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.8. <U>Primary Depository</U>.</B> Use Bank of America or Agent as its primary depository institution for all purposes, including the
maintenance of all collection accounts including the Subject Accounts, lockbox administration, funds transfer, information reporting services and other treasury management services, provided that this section shall not be interpreted in any manner
to prohibit other Credit Facilities permitted under<B> Section 10.2.2</B>.<B> </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.9. <U>Canadian Pension Plans</U></B>. For each
existing, or hereafter adopted, Canadian Pension Plan, each Obligor will, and will cause each Subsidiary to, in a timely fashion comply with and perform in all material respects all of its obligations under and in respect of such Canadian Pension
Plan, including under any funding agreements and all Applicable Laws, unless any failure to so comply or perform could not reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect.<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.1.10. <U>Post-Closing Covenants</U>. </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B></B>(a) Within thirty (30)&nbsp;days after the Restatement Effective Date (or such later date as Agent may determine in its sole
discretion), with respect to the Canadian Guarantor, comply or cause compliance with <B>Section&nbsp;8.2.4 </B>and<B> Section 8.5</B>.<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(b) Within thirty (30)&nbsp;days after the Restatement Effective Date (or such later date as Agent may determine in its sole discretion),
deliver to Agent certificates of insurance for the insurance policies carried by the Canadian Guarantor together with evidence satisfactory to Agent that Agent is named as loss payee (either through separate endorsement or through the terms of such
insurance policies) with respect to Collateral, all in compliance with the Loan Documents. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2. <U>Negative Covenants</U>.</B> As
long as any Commitments or Obligations are outstanding, each Obligor shall not:<B> </B></P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-67- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.1. <U>Permitted Debt</U>.</B> Permit any Subsidiary that is party to any Specified
Supplier Agreement or any Subsidiary Inventory Agreement to create, incur, guarantee or suffer to exist any Borrowed Money, except:<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(a) Borrowed Money owed to any Obligor to the extent the loans by such Obligor in such Subsidiary were permitted to be made hereunder; and
</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(b) Borrowed Money in an amount not to exceed $10,000,000 for any such Subsidiary and $20,000,000 in the aggregate for all such
Subsidiaries. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.2. <U>Permitted Liens</U></B>. <B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(a) Create or suffer to exist (or permit to exist) any Lien upon (1)&nbsp;Accounts, Inventory, Subject Accounts, Dominion Account, or the
proceeds thereof, except Permitted Liens described in clauses (i), (iii), (iv), (vii), or (ix)&nbsp;below; (2)&nbsp;any assets of any Subsidiary that is party to any Specified Supplier Agreement or any Subsidiary Inventory Agreement if such Lien
secures any obligations of such Subsidiary for Borrowed Money; or (3)&nbsp;any of its Property (other than Accounts or Inventory or the proceeds thereof) at any time when the Payment Conditions are not satisfied, except with respect to this clause
(3)&nbsp;the following (collectively, &#147;<U>Permitted Liens</U>&#148;): </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(i) (A) Liens in favor of Agent and (B)&nbsp;Liens in favor
of other providers of Credit Facilities (or their representatives) so long as (1)&nbsp;if such Credit Facilities are secured by any Collateral, then (x)&nbsp;such Liens are subject to an Intercreditor Agreement and (y)&nbsp;Liens on any Collateral
must be junior to the Liens on Collateral securing the Obligations, and (2)&nbsp;the aggregate principal amount of all such Debt under such Credit Facilities (including under this Agreement) at any one time outstanding does not exceed
$1,500,000,000; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(ii) Purchase Money Liens securing Permitted Purchase Money Debt; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(iii) Liens for Taxes not yet due or being Properly Contested; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(iv) (A) statutory Liens (other than Liens for Taxes or imposed under ERISA) on Collateral arising in the Ordinary Course of Business, but
only if (1)&nbsp;payment of the obligations secured thereby is not yet due or is being Properly Contested, and (2)&nbsp;such Liens do not materially impair the value or use of the Property or materially impair operation of the business of any
Obligor or Subsidiary and (B)&nbsp;statutory Liens on Property (other than Collateral) that could not reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(v) Liens incurred or deposits made in the Ordinary Course of Business to secure the performance of tenders, bids, leases, contracts (except
those relating to Borrowed Money), statutory obligations and other similar obligations, or arising as a result of progress payments under government contracts, as long as such Liens are, to the extent relating to the Collateral, at all times junior
to Agent&#146;s Liens; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(vi) Liens arising in the Ordinary Course of Business that are subject to Lien Waivers; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(vii) (A) Liens on Collateral arising by virtue of a judgment or judicial order against any Obligor, or any Property of an Obligor, as long
as such Liens are (1)&nbsp;in existence for less than 20 consecutive days or being Properly Contested, and (2)&nbsp;at all times junior to Agent&#146;s Liens and (B)&nbsp;Liens on Property (other than Collateral) arising by virtue of a judgment or
judicial order against any Obligor, or any Property of an Obligor, as long as such Liens, judgment, or judicial order could not reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect; </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-68- </P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(viii) easements, rights-of-way, restrictions, covenants or other agreements of record, and
other similar charges or encumbrances on Real Estate, that do not secure any material monetary obligation and do not interfere with the Ordinary Course of Business; </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(ix) normal and customary rights of setoff upon deposits in favor of depository institutions, and Liens of a collecting bank on Payment Items
in the course of collection; </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(x) other Liens securing obligations that are less than $25,000,000 in the aggregate as long as such Liens
could not reasonably be expected to have a Material Adverse Effect; and </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(xi) existing Liens shown on Schedule 10.2.2 or Liens incurred
at a time when the Payment Conditions were satisfied immediately before and immediately after giving effect to such incurrence, as applicable. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(b) Permit any Material Debt (other than Purchase Money Debt) to be secured by any Property of the Obligors unless (i)&nbsp;with respect to
Material Debt that is secured in whole or in part by any Collateral, (A)&nbsp;the Obligors shall have provided Agent at least 30 days written notice prior to such Material Debt becoming so secured and (B)&nbsp;such Material Debt is subject to an
Intercreditor Agreement, and (ii)&nbsp;with respect to Material Debt that is secured by Property (other than Collateral), (A)&nbsp;the Obligors shall have provided Agent at least 30 days written notice prior to such Material Debt becoming so secured
and (B)&nbsp;if required by Agent in its reasonable discretion, the holders of such Material Debt (or their representatives) shall have entered into an agreement, in form and substance reasonably satisfactory to Agent, granting Agent access rights
to any Collateral that may from time to time come into the possession or control of such holders (or their representatives) or to any location securing such Material Debt where any such Collateral may be located from time to time. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.3. <U>Amendments to Subordinated Debt; Material Debt; Subsidiary Inventory Agreements</U>.</B> <B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(a) Amend, supplement or otherwise modify any document, instrument or agreement relating to any Subordinated Debt, if such amendment,
supplement, or modification (a)&nbsp;increases the principal balance of such Debt, increases any required payment of principal, or increases any required payment of interest in an amount greater than 500 basis points; (b)&nbsp;accelerates the date
on which any installment of principal or any interest is due, or adds any additional redemption, put or prepayment provisions; (c)&nbsp;shortens the final maturity date or otherwise accelerates amortization; (d)&nbsp;increases or adds any material
periodic fees or charges; or (e)&nbsp;modifies any covenant in a manner or adds any representation, covenant or default that in any such case is more onerous or restrictive in any material respect for any Obligor or Subsidiary, or that is otherwise
materially adverse to any Obligor, any Subsidiary or Lenders. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(b) Amend, supplement or otherwise modify any agreement or instrument in
respect of Material Debt if such amendment, supplement, or modification results in the Obligations not constituting permitted indebtedness thereunder, results in the Liens securing the Obligations not constituting permitted liens thereunder, or
otherwise prohibits any Obligor from performing its obligations under the Loan Documents. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">(c) Amend, supplement or otherwise modify,
directly or indirectly, any Subsidiary Inventory Agreement or the terms thereof without the prior written consent of Agent. </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-69- </P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.4. <U>Distributions; Upstream Payments</U>.</B> As to Parent, declare or make any
Distributions (other than non-cash distributions of Equity Interests) unless (a)&nbsp;the Payment Conditions are satisfied immediately before and immediately after giving effect to such declaration or Distribution, provided that if the Payment
Conditions are satisfied immediately before and immediately after giving effect to the declaration of a Distribution (assuming for the purposes of the calculation thereof that all such declared Distributions have been made), then the Payment
Conditions with respect thereto shall not be required to be satisfied at the time of the actual Distribution so long as such actual distribution occurs within 30 days after such declaration and (b)&nbsp;immediately before and immediately after
giving effect to such Distribution, Net Worth shall be greater than zero; or create or suffer to exist any encumbrance or restriction on the ability of a Subsidiary to make any Upstream Payment, except for encumbrances or restrictions under
Applicable Law or in effect on the Restatement Effective Date or first put into place at a time when the Payment Conditions were satisfied. <B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.5. <U>Restricted Investments</U>.</B> Make any Restricted Investment.<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.6. <U>Disposition of Assets</U>.</B> (a)&nbsp;make any Asset Disposition, except (i)&nbsp;a Permitted Asset Disposition, (ii)&nbsp;a
transfer of Property by an Obligor to an Obligor, (iii)&nbsp;a transfer of Property (other than Collateral, except as permitted by clause (c)&nbsp;below) by an Obligor to a Subsidiary, (iv)&nbsp;an Asset Disposition permitted by clause
(c)&nbsp;below, or (v)&nbsp;an Asset Disposition at a time when the Payment Conditions were satisfied immediately before and after giving effect thereto, (b)&nbsp;sell, factor, securitize, or otherwise transfer Accounts or any Obligor&#146;s rights
therein, or enter into any arrangement to do the foregoing, other than the granting of Liens or other transfers to the Agent pursuant to the Loan Documents, or (c)&nbsp;sell or otherwise transfer Inventory or any Obligor&#146;s rights therein, or
enter into any arrangement to do the foregoing, other than (i)&nbsp;the granting of Liens or other transfers to the Agent pursuant to the Loan Documents, (ii)&nbsp;the granting of Liens permitted by <B>Section&nbsp;10.2.2(a)(i)</B>, (iii)&nbsp;the
sale, assignment or other transfer of Inventory to customers in the Ordinary Course of Business, and (iv)&nbsp;the sale or transfer of Inventory to Subsidiaries of Parent in the Ordinary Course of Business so long as such Subsidiary is party to, and
such Inventory is subject to, a Subsidiary Inventory Agreement. <B> </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.7. <U>Loans</U>.</B> Make any loans or other advances of
money to any Person at a time when the Payment Conditions are not satisfied immediately before and immediately after giving effect to such loans or advances, except (a)&nbsp;advances to an officer or employee for salary, travel expenses, commissions
and similar items in the Ordinary Course of Business; (b)&nbsp;prepaid expenses and extensions of trade credit made in the Ordinary Course of Business; (c)&nbsp;deposits with financial institutions permitted hereunder; (d)&nbsp;as long as no Default
or Event of Default exists, intercompany loans by an Obligor to another Obligor; and (e)&nbsp;in connection with Investments not prohibited by <B>Section 10.2.5</B>.<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.8. <U>Restrictions on Payment of Certain Debt</U>.</B> Unless the Payment Conditions are satisfied immediately before and immediately
after giving effect to such payments, make any payments (whether voluntary or mandatory, or a prepayment, redemption, retirement, defeasance or acquisition) with respect to any (a)&nbsp;Subordinated Debt, except regularly scheduled payments of
principal, interest and fees, but only to the extent permitted under any subordination agreement relating to such Debt (and a Senior Officer or other Financial Reporting Officer of Parent shall certify to Agent, not less than five Business Days
prior to the date of payment, that all conditions under such agreement have been satisfied); or (b)&nbsp;Borrowed Money (other than (i)&nbsp;the Obligations, (ii)&nbsp;with respect to Material Debt Permitted Maturity Payments, or
(iii)&nbsp;refinancings made solely with proceeds from other Debt permitted to be incurred hereunder) prior to its due date under the agreements evidencing such Debt (or as amended thereafter with the consent of Agent).<B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:8%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>10.2.9. <U>Fundamental Changes</U>.</B> Change its name or conduct business under any fictitious name, change its tax, charter or other
organizational identification number, or change its form or <B> </B></P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center">-70- </P>
